Produces an exhaustive caller/consumer trace with file:line evidence, under the discipline that a grep hit is not proof the code is live. Runs before a gap is accepted or a scope is expanded. Strictly read-only. Output is a memo file only — never a contract, an issue close, or a PR. ([nifabulous/Relay](https://github.com/nifabulous/Relay/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/impact-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **impact-researcher**: Narrow planning research on change impact: for one named symbol, interface, config key, CLI flag, API field, or file format, finds everything that depends on it and what a proposed change would break — each dependent with path:line, classified by kind. Read-only; answers in a small structured file. Use in /task-pipeline research when a plan must size or order a change by its ripple. ([jsco2t/dotfiles](https://github.com/jsco2t/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/impact-researcher.md))
- **impact-reviewer**: Review-only review of the change itself and its IMPACT — correctness, regressions, security, migration/deploy safety, plus adjacent and dependent parts of the code. - **impact-reviewer**: Review code changes using parent-provided or self-generated candidate impact maps before source inspection. ([BadLiveware/pi](https://github.com/BadLiveware/pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/impact-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **impact-reviewer**: **Purpose:** Review possible CIA consequences and business interpretation of a path. - **impact-reviewer**: 変更の連鎖影響と破壊範囲を分析。依存モジュール、公開API変更、SPOF生成、循環依存を検出。 ([morodomi/dev-crew](https://github.com/morodomi/dev-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/impact-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **impact-reviewer**: Reviews a code change on the blast-radius axis only: whether the change breaks existing callers through altered signatures, return values, or error contracts, whether removed or renamed symbols still have references, and whether shared state, external API shapes, event payloads, or DB schemas are affected. Use as one lens of a multi-perspective review. Does not look at bugs inside a single function, module structure, style, or test coverage. - **impact-reviewer**: Cheap second look at a planned or finished change: lists files that usually change together with it (from git history and imports) and that the plan or diff leaves out. - **impl-l5**: Implements a Level 5 coding task — architecture decisions, or correctness that is subtle in concurrency, performance, or security. Use when getting it wrong is expensive to undo: lock ordering and data races, lifetime and aliasing rules, protocol or schema design, trust boundaries, hot-path memory layout. Do NOT use for routine multi-file work (use impl-l3-4). If this model tier is unavailable, fall back to impl-l3-4. ([coldmon91/custom4agent](https://github.com/coldmon91/custom4agent/blob/HEAD/agent_settings/agents/impl-l5.md))
- **impl-lead**: 实施总指挥。语言无关。调度 Agent Team 执行 Spec-Driven Incremental Deploy。 Phase 0 声明环境（含 tech-profile.yaml），Phase 1 搭建基础设施， Phase 2 功能循环（RED→GREEN→REVIEW→DEPLOY），Phase 3 端到端验证。 RALPH 外层循环自动驱动：Read→Analyze→List→Plan→Handle，直到所有功能完成。 所有语言特定命令从 tech-profile.yaml 读取。 ([infra403/agentic-engineering-lab](https://github.com/infra403/agentic-engineering-lab/blob/HEAD/claude-code/plugins/product-design-plugin/agents/impl-lead.md) · ★5)
- **impl-lead**: Implementation team lead. Takes open Issues — from either the research team or the review team — decomposes them into non-overlapping file-ownership units, returns the plan to the manager for approval, then spawns one implementation teammate per independent unit in its own git worktree. Returns each opened PR to the manager as ready for review (the review team reviews and merges it, in rounds run by the manager), reworks it when asked, and reports the PRs, verdicts and release to the manager in its final report. ([Ribhu-Labs/teamflow](https://github.com/Ribhu-Labs/teamflow/blob/HEAD/agents/impl-lead.md))

- **implementador**: Escribe pruebas y código mínimo contra una especificación humana aprobada, sin autocertificarse. ([lgzarturo/microsdd](https://github.com/lgzarturo/microsdd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Escreve o código do plano aprovado. SQL, React, lógica de domínio. Nada além do plano. Use depois que a pesquisa e o desenho estiverem prontos. ([manozita/maccs-caixa](https://github.com/manozita/maccs-caixa/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))

- **implementador**: Agente Implementador Full-Stack por Rebanadas Verticales (Vertical Slicing + TDD). - **implementador**: Implements one approved plan chunk with its own files, in parallel with others. Use only when there are independent chunks; linear or small work is done by the main agent. ([nachopalmeri/agents-system](https://github.com/nachopalmeri/agents-system/blob/HEAD/config/generated/gemini/agents/implementador.md) · 3 copies)
- **implementador**: Implementa un cambio OpenSpec ya aprobado, en su propia rama, con tests, y abre el PR. Usar con el id del cambio. ([netxgen-2050/lighthouse-platform](https://github.com/netxgen-2050/lighthouse-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Implementa o plano solicitado pelo agente principal no FalaFácil, você pode executar apenas os testes que você mesmo escrever. ([OthonBreener/falafacil](https://github.com/OthonBreener/falafacil/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Implementa features y cambios multi-archivo siguiendo los estándares de Alyp Studio. Despachalo (desde el loop orquestador) para trabajo de implementación que NO requiere decisiones de arquitectura ni seguridad crítica; con override model "opus" para debugging difícil (heisenbug, race, cross-system). Cascada local OBLIGATORIA — delega las sub-tareas mecánicas al ejecutor local (tier light por default; heavy solo si requiere razonamiento; mapeo en ~/.claude/capacity.yaml). ([pabloRB25/coding_practices_alyp](https://github.com/pabloRB25/coding_practices_alyp/blob/HEAD/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Implementa um pacote delimitado (código Luau, Blender, scripts) com arquivos reservados em docs/TAREFAS.md. Use para o grosso da execução; o coordenador (Opus) planeja e revisa. ([peluca2007/Moshoku-roblox](https://github.com/peluca2007/Moshoku-roblox/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Implementa un grupo de requisitos de un SPEC.md — código y pruebas — siguiendo la arquitectura del proyecto. Usar cuando haya requisitos EARS concretos que convertir en código. No usar para decidir qué hacer ni para revisar lo hecho. ([pjimenez2510/clinica-api](https://github.com/pjimenez2510/clinica-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Implementa una unidad de trabajo acotada de un plan aprobado a partir de un encargo autocontenido — reutilizando antes de crear, sin salirse del encargo — y la verifica. Úsalo solo para tareas marcadas [paralelizable] con ficheros disjuntos (p. ej. backend y frontend con el contrato fijado) o para cambios mecánicos amplios. No lo uses para trabajo secuencial o que dependa de la conversación: eso lo implementa la sesión principal. Nunca hace commits ni instala dependencias. ([QudeCode/ai-bank](https://github.com/QudeCode/ai-bank/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Construye UNA tarea del plan aprobado, en index.html, y se detiene. También marca la tarea cuando el humano confirma y corrige el código cuando la validación falla. NUNCA cambia la spec ni el plan, salvo marcar la tarea. ([RaulChihuaicura/sdd-agents](https://github.com/RaulChihuaicura/sdd-agents/blob/HEAD/kit/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Solo dentro de una orquestación que el usuario inició con /orquesta:arquitecto (existe .orquesta/PLAN.md); fuera de ella no lo elijas por tu cuenta. Implementa exactamente una tarea descrita en un BRIEF de .orquesta/briefs (código .NET/C#, SQL, PowerShell, tests). Tier estándar del protocolo orquesta. Usalo cuando el arquitecto ya escribió el BRIEF con contrato y criterios de aceptación; no para explorar ni para decidir arquitectura. - **implementador**: Worker for one well-scoped work package the coordinator assigns (a ROADMAP item, an audit-remediation task, a multi-step change). Receives exact paths and exit criteria, implements it, commits and pushes as it goes. Use for every delegated package; the coordinator never uses Opus for these. ([salvamalfa/aeromexico-tracker](https://github.com/salvamalfa/aeromexico-tracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Implementador de código. Usar para ejecutar un plan ya definido (del agente planificador o de la persona), aplicar cambios acotados y bien especificados, escribir pruebas y corregir fallas de lint, tipos o pruebas. No toma decisiones de diseño: si el plan es ambiguo o contradice el código, se detiene y lo reporta. ([segurolotengopy/ChatbotRAG](https://github.com/segurolotengopy/ChatbotRAG/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Use este agente para executar UMA tarefa já diagnosticada e aprovada por humano, dentro de escopo de arquivos explicitamente autorizado. - **implementador**: Implementación acotada (feature/fix de 1-5 archivos con alcance claro). Modelo de código equilibrado. ([Turing0x/relevo](https://github.com/Turing0x/relevo/blob/HEAD/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Ficha del worker `implementador` del orquestador. Términos en [../GLOSSARY.md](../GLOSSARY.md). Rutas absolutas desde `~/.claude/`. ([Turing0x/relevo](https://github.com/Turing0x/relevo/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/implementador.md))
- **implementador**: Desenvolvedor chamado pelo /vo:implementar. Recebe uma sub-task do VO, as regras da spec que ela cobre e o conteúdo das skills do projeto que valem para os arquivos; escreve o código de produção seguindo essas skills e o jeito do código existente, roda a compilação e devolve um relatório com os arquivos, as decisões e as dúvidas. - **implementador**: Executa implementação de código a partir de delegações da sessão principal. - **implementador-backend**: Implementador backend do fluxo de entrega. - **implementador-frontend**: Implementa uma tarefa de tela/componente de apresentação frontend (React), sem ciclo de TDD, seguindo as convenções de frontend do projeto. - **implementador-frontend**: Planeja e implementa o escopo de uma Issue no frontend React do SEMA ALERTA (telas, componentes, rotas, Axios, mocks MSW, gráficos, mapa e testes Vitest), corrige achados e registra o diário. Não use para laços longos (muitos passos encadeados, processos em segundo plano, depuração aberta): divida a tarefa ou use implementador-sonnet. - **implementador-rapido**: Tareas mecánicas y bien acotadas - renombrar, mover, limpiar código o CSS sin uso, generar boilerplate o pruebas siguiendo un ejemplo del repositorio.
